FFmpeg bundled component source and build information
=====================================================

Binary: ffmpeg version n7.1.5-12-g1fdbca85aa-20260805 Copyright (c) 2000-2026 the FFmpeg developers
License: LGPL-3.0-or-later (see LICENSE.txt)
Binary release: https://github.com/BtbN/FFmpeg-Builds/releases/download/autobuild-2026-08-05-15-18/ffmpeg-n7.1.5-12-g1fdbca85aa-win64-lgpl-shared-7.1.zip

Build scripts and exact build recipe: https://github.com/BtbN/FFmpeg-Builds/tree/autobuild-2026-08-05-15-18
Corresponding FFmpeg source revision: https://github.com/FFmpeg/FFmpeg/tree/1fdbca85aa

This product uses the dynamically linked LGPL build without libx264/libx265.
Video encoding is performed by the Windows Media Foundation h264_mf encoder.
The FFmpeg DLL files are installed next to ffmpeg.exe and may be replaced by
a compatible build in accordance with the LGPL license terms.
